        Idea:
Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs

## Overview

Phase 9 replaces the Phase 5 `author test` scaffold (which only diffs pre-existing files) with a full runtime replay: it starts a task run in a temporary scratch directory, auto-approves all attested steps, executes code steps, captures the event stream, normalizes volatile fields, and diffs against a golden file. The existing scaffold tests (`test_author_test_scaffold.py`) must continue to pass because they test the Phase 5 behavior for missing/empty golden files — but the core `_cmd_test` function gains a new runtime replay path when conditions are met.

**Key architectural decision**: The replay runs in a temporary projects directory (scratch), completely isolated from real project state. Auto-approval is gated by `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Event normalization strips `ts`, `hash`, `run_id`, and absolute paths, keeping structural fields only.

## Phase 1: Event Normalization + Auto-Approval Foundation

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` — event normalization helper
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict) -> dict` that strips volatile fields (`ts`, `hash`, `run_id`, `pid`) and replaces absolute paths with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>` by anchoring on the run directory passed as a parameter.
2. **Implement** `normalize_events_jsonl(path: Path, run_dir: Path) -> list[dict]` that reads, normalizes each line, and returns the list.
3. **Decision on hash**: Strip `hash` entirely. Hash-chain hashes depend on `ts` and `prev_hash`, making them inherently volatile. Re-computing over normalized form would require replaying the chain, which adds complexity without benefit — the structural fields alone are sufficient for diffing.
4. **Keep structural fields**: `kind`, `plan_step_id`, `plan_step_path`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `check_id`, `produces_name`, `cas_sha256`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `reason`, `on_exhaust`, `max_iterations`, `child_plan_hash`, `actor`, `plan_hash`, `item_ids`.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval guard to `artagents/core/task/gate.py`
**Scope:** Small, modify `_dispatch_attested`

1. **Add env check** at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (after `match_attested_command` succeeds): when `os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1"`, skip identity validation entirely and use synthetic attestor: `attestor_kind="agent"`, `attestor_id="author_test"`, `evidence=()`. Add `"source": "author_test"` to the event payload.
2. The env guard is checked ONLY in `_dispatch_attested`, which is only reachable through `gate_command` (used by both `pipeline.py` main dispatch and `lifecycle_ack._ack_approve`). In normal `start` flow, `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` is not set, so this is inert.

### Step 3: Create `artagents/orchestrate/author_test_runner.py` — runtime replay engine
**Scope:** Medium, new file

1. **Implement** `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json: dict, fixture_dir: Path, packs_root: Path) -> Path`:
   a. Create a temporary projects root via `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="author_test_")`.
   b. Choose a project slug (e.g., `author_test_smoke`).
   c. Write the plan JSON to `<projects_root>/<slug>/plan.json`.
   d. Compute plan hash, generate run_id, write `active_run.json` with `run_id` and `plan_hash`.
   e. Create `runs/<run_id>/events.jsonl` with a `run_started` event.
   f. Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` in the process environment.
   g. **Loop**: call `peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, ...)`. If exhausted, break. If `CodeStep`: dispatch via `gate_command(slug, step.command, ...)`, run the subprocess with `subprocess.run()`, call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode)`. If `AttestedStep`: construct synthetic command `f"{step.command} --agent author_test"`, call `gate_command(slug, synthetic_command, ...)`.
   h. Copy fixture files into the step produces directories before each step runs (so the step's subprocess can find its inputs).
   i. Return the path to `events.jsonl`.

2. **Cleanup**: use `try/finally` to remove the scratch directory (or leave it if an env var `ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH=1` is set for debugging).

3. **Subprocess execution**: For code steps, run via `subprocess.run(cmd_parts, cwd=step_dir, env=env, capture_output=True)`. The env must include `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID`, `ARTAGENTS_TASK_PROJECT`, `ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID` so child processes attach to the task run.

## Phase 2: Wire `author test` CLI

### Step 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Medium, replace Phase 5 scaffold logic

1. **Replace** the current `_cmd_test` body (which reads pre-existing golden and captured files) with:
   a. Resolve the orchestrator and compile to JSON (or load from build).
   b. Load the fixture directory at `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` (must exist and not be empty).
   c. Call `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json, fixture_dir, packs_root)` to get the captured `events.jsonl` path.
   d. Normalize captured events using `normalize_events_jsonl`.
   e. Load golden from `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
   f. **If `--regenerate`**: write the normalized captured events as the new golden file, print confirmation, return 0.
   g. **Otherwise**: normalize golden, diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.

2. **Preserve Phase 5 behavior for missing/empty golden**: When golden is missing or empty AND `--regenerate` is NOT set, print a message saying "run with --regenerate to capture the first golden", return 2. This keeps `test_author_test_scaffold.py` tests passing.

3. **Add `--regenerate` flag** to the argparse test subparser.

## Phase 3: Golden Fixture for Hype Orchestrator

### Step 5: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py` — a minimal orchestrate-DSL plan for the hype pipeline
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Implement** a minimal `builtin.hype` orchestrator using the DSL — mirroring the design doc section 13 canonical example but simplified for testability: one code step that echoes something (no actual LLM calls needed for a smoke test). Alternatively, use the existing `run.py`-style execution wrapped in a code step.
2. Since this is a phase 9 golden test, the plan must be realistic but fast. A single `code` step that creates a file and passes an inline check is the simplest valid smoke test.

### Step 6: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` and `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`
**Scope:** Small, new files

1. The fixture directory can be minimal — just a `.keep` file or a stub input.
2. Run `author test --regenerate` once to produce the initial golden file, then commit it.

## Phase 4: Tests

### Step 7: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Create a temp pack with a simple code-step orchestrator, a fixture dir, run `author test`, assert exit 0 against a pre-written golden.

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert `author test` reports drift with unified diff and exits 1.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Run with `--regenerate`, assert the golden file is rewritten and matches the captured events.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Create a plan with an attested step. Run the replay. Assert the `events.jsonl` contains a `step_attested` event with `attestor_id: "author_test"` and `source: "author_test"`. Assert that WITHOUT `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, the attested step blocks.

### Step 11: Verify existing tests pass
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — these test the Phase 5 scaffold behavior (missing/empty golden -> exit 2). Our `_cmd_test` rewrite must preserve this behavior.
2. **Run** full `pytest tests/` to ensure no regressions.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (normalize.py) — foundation, no side effects
2. Step 2 (gate auto-approval) — small targeted change, tests first
3. Step 3 (runner.py) — core replay engine
4. Step 4 (CLI rewrite) — wires it all together
5. Steps 5-6 (hype golden fixture) — canonical fixture
6. Steps 7-10 (new tests) — prove Phase 9
7. Step 11 (regression) — final verification

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — Phase 5 tests must still pass (cheapest check after Step 4)
2. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py tests/test_author_test_drift.py tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — new Phase 9 tests
3. `pytest tests/` — full suite
4. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — manual acceptance

        Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revised)

## Overview

Phase 9 replaces the Phase 5 `author test` scaffold (file-vs-file diff of pre-written captured files) with a **runtime replay path**: the CLI starts a task run in a temporary scratch projects directory, auto-approves attested steps, executes code steps via subprocess, captures the event stream, normalizes volatile fields, and diffs against a golden file. The **Phase 5 scaffold path is retained** as a backward-compatible fallback: when no compiled plan exists at `<pack>/build/<orchestrator>.json`, the existing file-diff behavior runs against pre-written fixture captures — so the Phase 5 scaffold tests (`test_author_test_scaffold.py`) continue to pass unchanged.

**Key decisions from critique resolution**:
- **CLI signature**: `author test` switches from positional `qualified_id` to `--pack`/`--orchestrator` flags (matches the stop-condition acceptance command).
- **Orchestrator collision**: We create a new pack `builtin_golden` with a DSL orchestrator `hype` — avoids collision with the existing YAML-defined `builtin.hype` folder orchestrator at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/orchestrator.yaml`.
- **Auto-approval gate**: Bypass inserted in `validate_attested_identity` (gate.py) — scoped to only short-circuit identity validation when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` AND the agent/actor value is `author_test`. The synthetic command uses `--agent author_test` or `--actor author_test` based on the step's `ack.kind`.
- **Fixture inputs**: Fixture files go into `fixtures/<name>/` and are copied by the runner into the scratch project's run directory (not into produces dirs). Steps access them via an `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` env var.
- **Reuse existing runner**: The runtime replay uses `cmd_start` + a loop calling `cmd_next` (for code steps) and `cmd_ack` (for attested steps) — reusing the production entry points, not reimplementing the peek/dispatch/record loop.
- **`_strip_volatile` → normalize.py**: `_strip_volatile` is retired; `_cmd_test` imports from the new `normalize.py` instead. The old helper is removed from cli.py.
- **`sys.executable`**: All subprocess invocations use `sys.executable`, not hardcoded `python3`.
- **Env propagation**: `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` is propagated to child subprocesses via `child_subprocess_env`.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Normalization + Auto-Approval

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` — event normalization
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, run_dir: Path) -> dict`:
   - Strip volatile fields: `ts`, `hash`, `run_id`.
   - Replace absolute paths anchored at `run_dir` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`. For any string value that starts with `str(run_dir)`, replace the prefix. Also replace `str(run_dir.parent)` (the project root) with `<PROJECT_DIR>`.
   - Strip `pid` if present.
   - Keep all structural fields: `kind`, `plan_step_id`, `plan_step_path`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `check_id`, `produces_name`, `cas_sha256`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `reason`, `on_exhaust`, `max_iterations`, `child_plan_hash`, `actor`, `plan_hash`, `item_ids`, `source`.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_jsonl(path: Path, run_dir: Path) -> list[dict]`:
   - Read lines, JSON-decode each, pass through `normalize_event`, return list.

3. **Implement** `normalize_line(line: str, run_dir: Path) -> str`:
   - Thin wrapper that JSON-decodes, normalizes, re-encodes with `sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False`. Returns raw line on decode failure (so malformed lines surface in diff).

4. **Document**: Hash-chain hashes are stripped (not recomputed) because they depend on `ts` which is volatile. Structural fields alone are sufficient for diffing event drift.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval bypass to `validate_attested_identity` in `artagents/core/task/gate.py`
**Scope:** Tiny, modify existing function

1. **Add early return** at the top of `validate_attested_identity` (after the `--agent`/`--actor` presence check, before ack.kind-specific logic):
   ```python
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       if args.agent == "author_test" or args.actor == "author_test":
           kind = "agent" if args.agent == "author_test" else "actor"
           return kind, "author_test"
   ```
   This gates auto-approval on BOTH the env var AND the synthetic identity `author_test`, so accidental env leakage from a parent shell cannot bypass identity validation unless the command also carries the `author_test` identity.

2. **Propagate env to children**: In `artagents/core/task/env.py`, add `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to the `child_subprocess_env` function's key list so code-step subprocesses that may dispatch nested attested sub-steps inherit the auto-approval env.

## Phase 2: Runtime Replay Engine

### Step 3: Create `artagents/orchestrate/author_test_runner.py` — runtime replay
**Scope:** Medium, new file (~250 lines)

1. **Implement** `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json: dict, fixture_dir: Path, pack: str, name: str, packs_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]`:
   - Returns `(events_jsonl_path, run_dir)`.
   - Create temp directory via `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="author_test_")` as the projects root.
   - Use project slug `author_test_smoke` (validated).
   - Write `plan.json` to `<projects_root>/<slug>/plan.json`.
   - Call `cmd_start` with `["builtin_golden.hype", "--project", slug]` (or the actual pack.name). This writes `active_run.json`, creates run dir, writes `run_started` event.
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` in `os.environ` and restore on cleanup.
   - Copy fixture files from `fixture_dir` to `<run_dir>/fixture_inputs/` and set `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` env var.

2. **Drive the plan loop**:
   - Read `active_run.json` to get `run_id`.
   - Loop:
     a. Call `cmd_next` with `["--project", slug]` (stdout/stderr captured).
     b. Parse the output to determine what kind of step we're on:
        - If output contains `"run complete"` → break (exhausted).
        - If output contains `"run: <command>"` → CodeStep. Extract the command, run via `subprocess.run(sys.executable, "-c", "...")` with `cwd=<run_dir>`, then loop (the next `cmd_next` will advance the cursor).
        - If output contains ack instructions (the `AttestedStep` path) → AttestedStep. Build ack args: determine `ack.kind` from the step (agent or actor), use `--agent author_test` or `--actor author_test` accordingly. Call `cmd_ack` with `[step_path, "--project", slug, "--decision", "approve", "--agent", "author_test"]` (or `--actor`).
   - The loop terminates when `cmd_next` prints "run complete".

3. **Cleanup**: Use `try/finally`:
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to original value.
   - If `ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH=1` is set, print the scratch path and skip deletion; otherwise `shutil.rmtree` the temp directory.

4. **Edge cases**:
   - If the plan has no steps, `cmd_start` + first `cmd_next` → "run complete" immediately.
   - If a code step prints its argv multiple times (reentry), only execute once. The gate handles reentry detection.
   - Attested step with `ack.kind=actor`: use `--actor author_test` and set `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=author_test` temporarily.

## Phase 3: Wire `author test` CLI

### Step 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` and update parser in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Medium, replace Phase 5 scaffold logic with dual-path

1. **Parser change** (`_build_parser`, ~line 468):
   - Replace `test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", ...)` with:
     ```python
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", required=True, help="pack name (e.g. 'builtin_golden')")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", required=True, help="orchestrator name (e.g. 'hype')")
     test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, help="fixture name (under <pack>/fixtures/)")
     test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", help="overwrite golden with current output")
     ```

2. **Dispatch** (in `main`, ~line 491):
   - Change from `return _cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, packs_root)` to:
     ```python
     return _cmd_test(args.pack, args.orchestrator, args.fixture, args.regenerate, packs_root)
     ```

3. **New `_cmd_test` signature and body**:
   ```python
   def _cmd_test(pack: str, name: str, fixture_name: str, regenerate: bool, packs_root) -> int
   ```
   - Resolve `root = _packs_root_arg(packs_root)`, `pack_root = root / pack`.
   - Check for compiled plan at `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json`.
   - **Path A: Runtime replay** (compiled plan exists):
     a. Load `plan_json = json.loads(build_path.read_text())`.
     b. Load fixture dir at `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` (must exist).
     c. Call `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json, fixture_dir, pack, name, root)` → `(events_path, run_dir)`.
     d. Normalize captured events: `captured = normalize_events_jsonl(events_path, run_dir)`.
     e. Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     f. If `--regenerate`:
        - Write `captured` as normalized JSON lines to golden path.
        - Print: "regenerated golden at <golden_path> — commit this file if the change is intentional".
        - Return 0.
     g. If golden missing/empty AND not `--regenerate`:
        - Print: "no golden at <golden_path>; run with --regenerate to capture the first golden".
        - Return 2.
     h. Otherwise: load golden, normalize (using the same run_dir for path replacement), diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.
   - **Path B: Phase 5 scaffold fallback** (no compiled plan; backward compat):
     - This path is reached when `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json` does not exist.
     - Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - Captured: `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/events.jsonl`.
     - Check golden exists and non-empty — if not, print Phase 9 message and return 2.
     - Check captured exists — if not, print message and return 2.
     - Diff using `normalize_line` (from normalize.py, not `_strip_volatile`). Return 0/1.

4. **Remove `_strip_volatile`**: Delete the function and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant (~lines 287-308). Import `normalize_line` from `normalize.py` instead.

## Phase 4: Golden Fixture for Smoke Test

### Step 5: Create `artagents/packs/builtin_golden/hype.py` — DSL smoke orchestrator
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Implement** a minimal DSL orchestrator in a NEW pack `builtin_golden` (no collision with `builtin/hype/`):
   ```python
   """Smoke-test orchestrator for Phase 9 golden runs."""
   from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, code, file_nonempty

   @orchestrator("builtin_golden.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code(
               "smoke_step",
               argv=[sys.executable, "-c", "import json, os; json.dump({'ok': True}, open(os.path.join(os.environ.get('ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR', '.'), 'out.json'), 'w'))"],
               produces={"out": file_nonempty()},
           ),
       ]
   ```
   - Uses `sys.executable` (portable).
   - Reads from `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` (fixture input location).
   - Writes a produces file that passes the inline `file_nonempty` check.

### Step 6: Create fixture and golden
**Scope:** Small, new files

1. **`artagents/packs/builtin_golden/fixtures/smoke/`**: Create `.keep` file (minimal fixture).
2. **Compile** the orchestrator: `artagents author compile builtin_golden.hype`.
3. **Regenerate golden**: `artagents author test --pack builtin_golden --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` → writes `artagents/packs/builtin_golden/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.
4. **Commit** the golden file.

## Phase 5: Tests

### Step 7: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Create a temp pack with a simple code-step orchestrator (compile it), a fixture dir, pre-write a golden. Run `author test --pack <pack> --orchestrator <name> --fixture f1`. Assert exit 0.
2. This test uses the runtime replay path (compiled plan exists).

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Same setup as pass test but corrupt the golden (change a structural field). Assert `author test` exits 1 with unified diff on stdout.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Run with `--regenerate` on a missing golden. Assert golden file is created and a subsequent test pass exits 0.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test 1**: Create a plan with an attested step (agent ack). Run with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Assert the `events.jsonl` contains `step_attested` with `attestor_id: "author_test"`.
2. **Test 2**: Same plan WITHOUT `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Assert the attested step blocks (the loop hangs or `cmd_next` output contains ack instructions that don't advance).
3. **Test 3**: Verify that `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` with a non-`author_test` identity does NOT bypass validation (the env var must pair with the synthetic identity).

### Step 11: Verify existing tests pass
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — these use `demo.app` which has no compiled plan, so they hit **Path B** (Phase 5 scaffold fallback). They must pass unchanged.
2. **Run** full `pytest tests/` to ensure no regressions.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (normalize.py) — foundation, no side effects
2. Step 2 (auto-approval gate + env propagation) — tiny targeted changes
3. Step 3 (runner.py) — core replay engine, uses cmd_start/cmd_next/cmd_ack
4. Step 4 (CLI rewrite + parser change + retire _strip_volatile) — wires it all together
5. Step 5-6 (builtin_golden pack + smoke fixture + compile + regenerate golden)
6. Step 7-10 (new Phase 9 tests)
7. Step 11 (regression — scaffold tests + full suite)

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — Phase 5 tests must still pass (Path B fallback)
2. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py tests/test_author_test_drift.py tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — new Phase 9 tests
3. `pytest tests/` — full suite
4. `artagents author test --pack builtin_golden --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — manual acceptance

    "idea": "Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).\n\nLAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.\n\nDRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.\n\n\nPHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):\n\n- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.\n- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).\n- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.\n\nEXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):\n- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.\n\nWHAT TO IMPLEMENT:\n\n1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):\n   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`\n   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.\n   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` \u2014 fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.\n   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).\n   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths \u2014 keep only structural fields).\n   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.\n   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.\n\n2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):\n   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.\n   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow \u2014 only `author test` sets the env var.\n\n3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):\n   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).\n   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.\n   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) \u2014 recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.\n\n4. Regenerate workflow:\n   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.\n   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.\n\n5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` \u2014 minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` \u2014 the expected event sequence.\n\n6. Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.\n- No new dependencies (stdlib only).\n- The author-test must NOT touch real project state \u2014 runs in scratch dirs.\n- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.\n\nSTOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.",

        Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 3)

## Overview

Phase 9 replaces the Phase 5 `author test` scaffold (file-vs-file diff of pre-written captured files) with a **runtime replay path**: the CLI starts a task run in a temporary scratch projects directory, auto-approves attested steps, executes code steps via subprocess, captures the event stream, normalizes volatile fields, and diffs against a golden file. The **Phase 5 scaffold path is retained** as a backward-compatible fallback: when no compiled plan exists at `<pack>/build/<name>.json`, the existing file-diff behavior runs against pre-written fixture captures — so the Phase 5 scaffold tests (`test_author_test_scaffold.py`) continue to pass unchanged.

**Key decisions from this revision**:
- **CLI backward compatibility**: The `qualified_id` positional argument is retained alongside new `--pack`/`--orchestrator` flags. The parser accepts *either* form (but not both). Scaffold tests continue to pass `["test", "demo.app", "--fixture", "f1"]` unmodified. The stop-condition command `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` also works.
- **Pack placement**: The smoke DSL orchestrator is created at `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` (a .py file alongside the existing `builtin/hype/` folder). `resolve_orchestrator` checks for `<pack_root>/<name>.py` — the folder does not shadow the `.py` file during resolution. The `_cmd_new` scaffolding check prevents *creating* the file via the CLI when the folder exists, but we create it by hand (documented in plan). This satisfies the literal stop-condition invocation `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype`.
- **Runner loop: `peek_current_step` not stdout parsing**: The runtime replay drives the plan loop by calling `peek_current_step` (the stable API shared by `cmd_next`/`cmd_status`) to determine the current step kind. For CodeStep: run the subprocess, call `record_dispatch_complete`. For AttestedStep: call `cmd_ack` with `--agent author_test`/`--actor author_test`/`--decision approve`. No stdout substring matching.
- **No double-write of plan.json**: The runner creates a temp packs_root with the pre-compiled `build/<name>.json`, then calls `cmd_start` normally. `cmd_start` reads from `<packs_root>/build/<name>.json` and writes to `<project>/plan.json` — no redundant pre-write.
- **Auto-approval gate**: Bypass inserted in `validate_attested_identity` (gate.py) — scoped to only short-circuit identity validation when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` AND the agent/actor value is `author_test`. Placement in gate.py is intentional: it's the single chokepoint for all identity validation (pipeline + lifecycle_ack). The `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` env var does NOT need to be set for actor-ack steps because the early return fires before the `task_actor_env() != args.actor` check.
- **`_strip_volatile` retired**: removed from cli.py; all normalization uses the new `normalize.py` module.

**Unchanged from prior revision**:
- Fixture inputs go into `fixtures/<name>/` and are copied by the runner into the scratch run directory.
- `sys.executable` for all subprocess invocations.
- `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` propagated to child subprocesses via `child_subprocess_env`.
- `--regenerate` rewrites the golden file.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Normalization + Auto-Approval

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` — event normalization
**Scope:** Small, new file (~100 lines)

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, run_dir: Path) -> dict`:
   - Strip volatile fields: `ts`, `hash`, `run_id`, `pid`.
   - Replace absolute paths anchored at `run_dir` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`. For any string value that starts with `str(run_dir)`, replace the prefix. Also replace `str(run_dir.parent)` (the project root) with `<PROJECT_DIR>`.
   - Keep all structural fields: `kind`, `plan_step_id`, `plan_step_path`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `check_id`, `produces_name`, `cas_sha256`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `reason`, `on_exhaust`, `max_iterations`, `child_plan_hash`, `actor`, `plan_hash`, `item_ids`, `source`.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_jsonl(path: Path, run_dir: Path) -> list[dict]`:
   - Read lines, JSON-decode each, pass through `normalize_event`, return list.

3. **Implement** `normalize_line(line: str, run_dir: Path) -> str`:
   - Thin wrapper that JSON-decodes, normalizes, re-encodes with `sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False`. Returns raw line on decode failure (so malformed lines surface in diff).

4. **Document**: Hash-chain hashes are stripped (not recomputed) because they depend on `ts` which is volatile. Structural fields alone are sufficient for diffing event drift.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval bypass to `validate_attested_identity` in `artagents/core/task/gate.py`
**Scope:** Tiny, modify existing function (~5 lines)

1. **Add early return** AFTER the `--agent`/`--actor` presence checks (after line 1074, before `ack.kind`-specific logic at line 1075):
   ```python
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       if args.agent == "author_test" or args.actor == "author_test":
           kind = "agent" if args.agent == "author_test" else "actor"
           return kind, "author_test"
   ```
   This gates auto-approval on BOTH the env var AND the synthetic identity `author_test`, so accidental env leakage from a parent shell cannot bypass identity validation unless the command also carries the `author_test` identity. The `task_actor_env() != args.actor` check at line 1082 is never reached for actor-ack steps under this bypass, so `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` does not need to be set.

2. **Propagate env to children**: In `artagents/core/task/env.py`, add `"ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` to the key list in `child_subprocess_env` (alongside `TASK_RUN_ID_ENV` et al.) so code-step subprocesses that may dispatch nested attested sub-steps inherit the auto-approval env.

## Phase 2: Runtime Replay Engine

### Step 3: Create `artagents/orchestrate/author_test_runner.py` — runtime replay
**Scope:** Medium, new file (~200 lines)

1. **Implement** `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json: dict, fixture_dir: Path, pack: str, name: str, packs_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]`:
   - Returns `(events_jsonl_path, run_dir)`.
   - Create temp directory via `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="author_test_")` as the projects root.
   - Create a temp packs_root under the scratch dir: `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/build/<name>.json` — write the compiled `plan_json` there. Also create `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` with a `.keep` marker (the pack dir must exist for `cmd_start` to resolve).
   - Use project slug `author_test_smoke` (validated).
   - Call `cmd_start(["builtin.hype", "--project", slug], packs_root=<scratch_packs>, projects_root=<scratch_projects>)`. This writes `active_run.json`, `plan.json`, creates run dir, writes `run_started` event.
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` in `os.environ` and restore on cleanup.
   - Copy fixture files from `fixture_dir` to `<run_dir>/fixture_inputs/` and set `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` env var pointing there.

2. **Drive the plan loop** using `peek_current_step` + `cmd_ack`:
   - Load `plan.json` and `events.jsonl` from the project.
   - Read `active_run.json` to get `run_id`.
   - Loop:
     a. Load plan, read events, call `peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)`.
     b. If `peek.exhausted` or `peek.step is None` → break.
     c. If `isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep)`: run via `subprocess.run(peek.step.command_tokens)` with `cwd=<run_dir>`, `env=child_subprocess_env()`. Then call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode)` — need a `GateDecision` for this. Construct a minimal `GateDecision(active=True, run_id=run_id, slug=slug, project_root=proj_root, plan_step_path=peek.path_tuple, plan_step_id=STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple), events_path=events_path, produces=peek.step.produces, iteration=peek.iteration, item_id=peek.item_id, step_kind="code")`.
     d. If `isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep)`: build ack args based on `peek.step.ack.kind`. If `agent`: call `cmd_ack([STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple), "--project", slug, "--decision", "approve", "--agent", "author_test"], projects_root=projects_root)`. If `actor`: call `cmd_ack([... , "--actor", "author_test"], projects_root=projects_root)`. Include `--item <id>` if `peek.item_id` is set.
     e. After each loop iteration, reload events (cmd_ack writes step_attested which advances the cursor).
   - The loop terminates when `peek.exhausted`.

3. **Cleanup**: Use `try/finally`:
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to original value.
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR`.
   - If `ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH=1` is set, print the scratch path and skip deletion; otherwise `shutil.rmtree` the temp directory.

4. **Edge cases**:
   - If the plan has no steps, `peek_current_step` returns exhausted immediately.
   - Code step re-entry: the gate's `gate_command` handles reentry detection; `cmd_next` already prints the reentry path. The runner doesn't call `cmd_next` — it uses `peek_current_step` + `record_dispatch_complete` + `cmd_ack`.
   - Attested step with `ack.kind=actor`: use `--actor author_test`. The early return in `validate_attested_identity` handles it; no `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` env needed.

## Phase 3: Wire `author test` CLI

### Step 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` and update parser in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Medium, replace Phase 5 scaffold logic with dual-path

1. **Parser change** (`_build_parser`, ~line 468):
   - Keep the existing `test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", ...)` for backward compat.
   - Add optional flags that form a mutually exclusive alternative:
     ```python
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None, help="pack name (e.g. 'builtin')")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None, help="orchestrator name (e.g. 'hype')")
     ```
     The `qualified_id` positional is now `nargs="?"` (optional), but if both `--pack`/`--orchestrator` are given, the positional must be absent. Validate in `_cmd_test` dispatch, not in argparse.
   - Keep `test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, ...)`.
   - Add `test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", ...)`.

2. **Dispatch** (in `main`, ~line 491):
   - If `args.pack` and `args.orchestrator` are both set: `qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"`.
   - Otherwise: use `args.qualified_id` (the positional).
   - Validate that exactly one form was used.
   - Pass `args.regenerate` through.
   - Signature: `return _cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, args.regenerate, packs_root)`.

3. **New `_cmd_test` signature and body**:
   ```python
   def _cmd_test(qid: str, fixture_name: str, regenerate: bool, packs_root: Optional[Path]) -> int
   ```
   - Resolve `root = _packs_root_arg(packs_root)`, `pack, name = _qualified_split(qid)`, `pack_root = root / pack`.
   - Check for compiled plan at `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json`.
   - **Path A: Runtime replay** (compiled plan exists):
     a. Load `plan_json = json.loads(build_path.read_text())`.
     b. Load fixture dir at `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` (must exist).
     c. Call `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json, fixture_dir, pack, name, root)` → `(events_path, run_dir)`.
     d. Normalize captured events: `captured = normalize_events_jsonl(events_path, run_dir)`.
     e. Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     f. If `--regenerate`:
        - Write `captured` as normalized JSON lines to golden path.
        - Print: "regenerated golden at <golden_path> — commit this file if the change is intentional".
        - Return 0.
     g. If golden missing/empty AND not `--regenerate`:
        - Print: "no golden at <golden_path>; run with --regenerate to capture the first golden".
        - Return 2.
     h. Otherwise: load golden, normalize (using the same run_dir for path replacement), diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.
   - **Path B: Phase 5 scaffold fallback** (no compiled plan):
     - This path is reached when `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json` does not exist.
     - Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - Captured: `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/events.jsonl`.
     - Check golden exists and non-empty — if not, print Phase 9 message and return 2.
     - Check captured exists — if not, print message and return 2.
     - Diff using `normalize_line` (from normalize.py, not `_strip_volatile`). Return 0/1.

4. **Remove `_strip_volatile`**: Delete the function and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant (~lines 287-308). Import `normalize_line` from `normalize.py` instead.

## Phase 4: Golden Fixture for Smoke Test

### Step 5: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` — DSL smoke orchestrator (alongside existing `builtin/hype/` folder)
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Implement** a minimal DSL orchestrator in the existing `builtin` pack:
   ```python
   """Smoke-test orchestrator for Phase 9 golden runs.

   Lives alongside builtin/hype/ (a legacy folder-pack with run.py).
   resolve_orchestrator looks for builtin/hype.py first — the folder
   does not shadow the .py module during importlib resolution.
   """
   import sys
   from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, code, file_nonempty

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code(
               "smoke_step",
               argv=[sys.executable, "-c", "import json, os; json.dump({'ok': True}, open(os.path.join(os.environ.get('ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR', '.'), 'out.json'), 'w'))"],
               produces={"out": file_nonempty()},
           ),
       ]
   ```
   - Uses `sys.executable` (portable).
   - Reads from `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` (fixture input location).
   - Writes a produces file that passes the inline `file_nonempty` check.

### Step 6: Create fixture and golden
**Scope:** Small, new files

1. **`artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/`**: Create `.keep` file (minimal fixture).
2. **`artagents/packs/builtin/golden/`**: Create directory if it doesn't exist.
3. **Compile** the orchestrator: `artagents author compile builtin.hype`.
4. **Regenerate golden**: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` → writes `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.
5. **Commit** the golden file.

## Phase 5: Tests

### Step 7: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Create a temp pack with a simple code-step orchestrator (compile it), a fixture dir, pre-write a golden via regenerate. Run `author test --pack <pack> --orchestrator <name> --fixture f1`. Assert exit 0.
2. This test uses the runtime replay path (compiled plan exists).

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Same setup as pass test but corrupt the golden (change a structural field). Assert `author test` exits 1 with unified diff on stdout.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test**: Run with `--regenerate` on a missing golden. Assert golden file is created and a subsequent test pass exits 0.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file

1. **Test 1**: Create a plan with an attested step (agent ack). Run with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Assert the `events.jsonl` contains `step_attested` with `attestor_id: "author_test"` and `attestor_kind: "agent"`.
2. **Test 2**: Same plan WITHOUT `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`. Assert the attested step blocks (cmd_next output contains ack instructions that don't auto-resolve).
3. **Test 3**: Verify that `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` with a non-`author_test` identity does NOT bypass validation (the env var must pair with the synthetic identity).

### Step 11: Verify existing tests pass
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — these use `["test", "demo.app", "--fixture", "f1"]` (positional qualified_id, no `--pack`/`--orchestrator`) and have no compiled plan → they hit **Path B** (Phase 5 scaffold fallback). They must pass unchanged because the parser retains the positional argument.
2. **Run** full `pytest tests/` to ensure no regressions.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (normalize.py) — foundation, no side effects
2. Step 2 (auto-approval gate + env propagation) — tiny targeted changes
3. Step 3 (runner.py) — core replay engine, uses peek_current_step + cmd_ack
4. Step 4 (CLI rewrite + parser backward-compat + retire _strip_volatile) — wires it all together
5. Step 5-6 (builtin/hype.py smoke orchestrator + fixture + compile + regenerate golden)
6. Step 7-10 (new Phase 9 tests)
7. Step 11 (regression — scaffold tests + full suite)

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py` — Phase 5 tests must still pass (Path B fallback via positional qualified_id)
2. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py tests/test_author_test_drift.py tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py` — new Phase 9 tests
3. `pytest tests/` — full suite
4. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — manual acceptance (literal stop-condition command)

  ],
  "questions": [
    "Should the smoke test orchestrator use a real subprocess (sys.executable -c '...') or a no-op? Using sys.executable is portable and makes the golden meaningful. Leaning toward sys.executable.",
    "For Path B (scaffold fallback): normalize_line from normalize.py handles the same volatile stripping as the old _strip_volatile but also strips run_id/pid/abs paths. Since Path B fixtures are pre-written with synthetic data, the extra stripping should be harmless \u2014 normalize_line is a superset of _strip_volatile.",
    "Should the builtin/hype.py smoke orchestrator be documented as a test-only artifact? It lives in the production packs directory, but it's a one-step no-op that only writes to fixture_inputs. Documenting it in a module docstring should suffice.",
    "For cleanup: should the scratch directory be cleaned up on success as well as failure? Yes \u2014 cleanup in 'finally' block, but leave it when ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH=1 for debugging."
  ],
  "success_criteria": [
    {

  "assumptions": [
    "The dual-path strategy (runtime replay when compiled plan exists at <pack>/build/<name>.json, Phase 5 scaffold fallback otherwise) preserves backward compatibility. The Phase 5 scaffold tests use 'demo.app' which has no compiled build artifact, so they naturally take Path B.",
    "Creating builtin/hype.py alongside the existing builtin/hype/ folder does not collide during resolution. resolve_orchestrator checks pack_root / f'{name}.py' \u2014 the folder does not shadow the .py. _cmd_new's collision check prevents scaffolding via CLI, but manual file creation is fine.",
    "peek_current_step and cmd_ack are importable and callable programmatically in-process. They accept a projects_root parameter, which the runner sets to the scratch temp dir.",
    "The auto-approval bypass in validate_attested_identity requires both ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1 AND the identity value 'author_test'. An env leak alone cannot bypass \u2014 the command must also carry the synthetic identity.",
    "For actor-ack steps, the early return in validate_attested_identity fires before the task_actor_env() check, so ARTAGENTS_ACTOR does not need to be set.",
    "Fixture inputs are copied to <run_dir>/fixture_inputs/ and the ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR env var points there. Steps read inputs from this directory.",
    "The canonical_event_json function (events.py) already strips 'hash' before computing; normalize.py strips it too but after the fact for comparison.",
    "The subprocess for code steps captures stdout/stderr and checks returncode \u2014 nonzero returncodes are recorded in the event stream via step_completed but do not halt the run.",
    "The --regenerate flag works even when golden doesn't exist yet, making it the canonical bootstrap mechanism.",
    "Pack discovery is directory-based with no allowlist. Any directory under artagents/packs/ with a .py file is discoverable via importlib.util.spec_from_file_location."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 38.54,
  "structure_warnings": []
}

  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 3,
    "idea": "Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).\n\nLAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.\n\nDRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.\n\n\nPHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):\n\n- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.\n- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).\n- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.\n\nEXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):\n- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.\n\nWHAT TO IMPLEMENT:\n\n1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):\n   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`\n   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.\n   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` \u2014 fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.\n   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).\n   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths \u2014 keep only structural fields).\n   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.\n   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.\n\n2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):\n   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.\n   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow \u2014 only `author test` sets the env var.\n\n3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):\n   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).\n   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.\n   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) \u2014 recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.\n\n4. Regenerate workflow:\n   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.\n   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.\n\n5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` \u2014 minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` \u2014 the expected event sequence.\n\n6. Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.\n- No new dependencies (stdlib only).\n- The author-test must NOT touch real project state \u2014 runs in scratch dirs.\n- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.\n\nSTOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.",

        Robustness level:

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```
