PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 4)

## Overview

Phase 9 delivers the `artagents author test` runtime replay path: a CLI verb that starts a task run in a temporary scratch projects directory, auto-approves attested steps, executes code steps via subprocess, captures the event stream, normalizes volatile fields, and diffs against a golden file. The Phase 5 scaffold path (file-diff of pre-written captured files at `fixtures/<name>/events.jsonl`) is retained as a backward-compatible fallback when no compiled plan exists at `<pack>/build/<name>.json`.

**Source-verified decisions from this revision**:
- `CodeStep.command` is a `str` (plan.py:60) — the runner must use `shlex.split(step.command)` for subprocess argv, never reference `.command_tokens` which does not exist.
- `_dispatch_code` (gate.py:790-860) emits `step_dispatched` (line 847), applies `ARTAGENTS_TASK_*` env vars (line 848), and handles reentry detection (lines 807-845). The runner MUST go through `gate_command` before running the subprocess — bypassing it loses `step_dispatched` emission, `apply_task_run_env`, and reentry semantics.
- `cmd_ack._ack_approve` (lifecycle_ack.py:205-214) constructs the incoming command as `peek.step.command` + `--agent <id>` (or `--actor`). `match_attested_command` (gate.py:1025-1061) strips identity tokens and compares the remainder to `step.command`. The flow is correct for programmatic invocation with `--agent author_test`.
- `artagents/packs/*/build/` is gitignored (.gitignore:60). The compiled `build/hype.json` must be regenerated on fresh checkout. The plan explicitly calls `artagents author compile builtin.hype` before the acceptance command.
- No `builtin/hype/` folder or `builtin/hype.py` exists currently — the DSL smoke orchestrator can be created cleanly without collision.
- `child_subprocess_env` (env.py:68-81) does not currently propagate `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`. Must add it to the key list.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Normalization + Auto-Approval

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` — event normalization
**Scope:** Small, new file (~120 lines)

1. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, run_dir: Path) -> dict`:
   - Strip volatile fields: `ts`, `hash`, `run_id`, `pid`.
   - Replace absolute paths anchored at `run_dir` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`. For any string value that starts with `str(run_dir)`, replace the prefix. Also replace `str(run_dir.parent)` (the project root) with `<PROJECT_DIR>`.
   - Keep all structural fields: `kind`, `plan_step_id`, `plan_step_path`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `check_id`, `produces_name`, `cas_sha256`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `reason`, `on_exhaust`, `max_iterations`, `child_plan_hash`, `actor`, `plan_hash`, `item_ids`, `source`, `path_tuple`.

2. **Implement** `normalize_events_jsonl(path: Path, run_dir: Path) -> list[dict]`:
   - Read lines, JSON-decode each, pass through `normalize_event`, return list.

3. **Implement** `normalize_line(line: str, run_dir: Path) -> str`:
   - Thin wrapper that JSON-decodes, normalizes, re-encodes with `sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False`. Returns raw line on decode failure (so malformed lines surface in diff).

4. **Document**: Hash-chain hashes (`hash` field) are stripped (not recomputed) because they depend on `ts` which is volatile. Structural fields alone are sufficient for diffing event drift. `ts` is stripped because it's a UTC timestamp that varies per run. `run_id` is stripped because the runner generates a fresh one each time.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval bypass to `validate_attested_identity` in `artagents/core/task/gate.py`
**Scope:** Tiny, modify existing function (~5 lines)

1. **Add early return** at line 1074 of gate.py (right after the mutually-exclusive check, before the `step.ack.kind` branch at line 1075):
   ```python
   # Auto-approval for author test: requires BOTH env var AND synthetic identity.
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       if args.agent == "author_test" or args.actor == "author_test":
           kind = "agent" if args.agent == "author_test" else "actor"
           return kind, "author_test"
   ```
   This double-gates auto-approval on both the env var AND the synthetic identity `author_test`, so accidental `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` leakage from a parent shell cannot bypass identity validation unless the command also carries the `author_test` identity. The `task_actor_env() != args.actor` check at line 1082 is never reached for actor-ack steps under this bypass, so `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` does not need to be set.

2. **Propagate env to children**: In `artagents/core/task/env.py`, add `"ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` to the key list in `child_subprocess_env` (line 71-77) alongside `TASK_RUN_ID_ENV` et al. so code-step subprocesses that may dispatch nested attested sub-steps inherit the auto-approval env.

## Phase 2: Runtime Replay Engine

### Step 3: Create `artagents/orchestrate/author_test_runner.py` — runtime replay
**Scope:** Medium, new file (~200 lines)

1. **Implement** `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json: dict, fixture_dir: Path, pack: str, name: str, packs_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]`:
   - Returns `(events_jsonl_path, run_dir)`.
   - Create temp directory via `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="author_test_")` as the projects root.
   - Inside the scratch dir, create `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/build/<name>.json` — write the compiled `plan_json` there. Also create `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/` directory (needed for `_resolve_packs_root` to find the pack).
   -ump to project slug `author_test_smoke` (validated per `validate_project_slug`).
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` in `os.environ` and restore on cleanup.
   - Save/restore original env values for both `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` and `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR`.
   - Copy fixture files from `fixture_dir` to `<run_dir>/fixture_inputs/` and set `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` env var pointing there (after `cmd_start` creates the run_dir).
   - The run_dir doesn't exist until `cmd_start` completes, so fixture copy must happen after `cmd_start` returns.

2. **Call `cmd_start`** (as imported from `artagents.core.task.lifecycle`):
   ```python
   from artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_start
   rc = cmd_start([f"{pack}.{name}", "--project", slug], packs_root=scratch_packs, projects_root=scratch_projects)
   if rc != 0:
       raise RuntimeError(f"cmd_start returned {rc}")
   ```
   `cmd_start` (lifecycle.py:130-220) reads the compiled plan from `<packs_root>/<pack>/build/<name>.json` (line 162), writes it to `<project>/plan.json` (line 179), creates run dir (line 198-199), writes `active_run.json` (line 206), and appends `run_started` event. The runner does NOT pre-write plan.json — `cmd_start` handles it entirely.

3. **Copy fixtures**: After `cmd_start` succeeds, read `active_run.json` to get `run_id`, compute `run_dir = project_dir / "runs" / run_id`, copy `fixture_dir/*` to `run_dir / "fixture_inputs" /`, set `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR"] = str(run_dir / "fixture_inputs")`.

4. **Drive the plan loop** using `peek_current_step` + `gate_command` + `cmd_ack`:
   -lloops variables: `proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=scratch_projects)`, `plan = load_plan(proj_root / "plan.json")`, `events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"`.
   - Loop:
     a. Reload `plan` and `events` from disk (events grow as steps complete).
     b. Call `peek = peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)` (gate.py:454-507).
     c. If `peek.exhausted` or `peek.step is None` → break.
     d. **If `isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep)`** (gate.py:58-63, `command` is a `str`):
        - Call `gate_command` with `command=peek.step.command` to emit `step_dispatched` and apply `ARTAGENTS_TASK_*` env vars:
          ```python
          decision = gate_command(slug, peek.step.command, [], root=scratch_projects)
          ```
          `gate_command` (gate.py:510-604) auto-traverses to the leaf, dispatches via `_dispatch_code` (line 574), which emits `step_dispatched` (line 847) and calls `apply_task_run_env` (line 848).
        - Run the subprocess using `shlex.split(peek.step.command)` (because `step.command` is a `str`, not a list — plan.py:60):
          ```python
          cmd_tokens = shlex.split(peek.step.command)
          proc = subprocess.run(cmd_tokens, cwd=run_dir, env=child_subprocess_env(), capture_output=True)
          ```
        - Call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, proc.returncode)` (gate.py:1096-1107) which emits `step_completed` (line 1104) and runs inline produces checks (line 1106-1107).
     e. **If `isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep)`** (gate.py:67-74):
        - Build ack args based on `peek.step.ack.kind`:
          - If `"agent"`: `ack_argv = [STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple), "--project", slug, "--decision", "approve", "--agent", "author_test"]`
          - If `"actor"`: `ack_argv = [..., "--actor", "author_test"]`
        - Include `--item <peek.item_id>` if `peek.item_id` is set.
        - Call `cmd_ack(ack_argv, projects_root=scratch_projects)` (lifecycle_ack.py:102-187).
          `cmd_ack`eps calls `peek_current_step` to verify cursor (line 158-173), then `_ack_approve` (line 190-228) which constructs the incoming command as `peek.step.command + " --agent author_test"` (line 205-214), calls `gate_command` (line 217), which routes to `_dispatch_attested` (gate.py:904-973), which calls `match_attested_command` (line 918) and `validate_attested_identity` (line 922). The auto-approval bypass in Step 2 short-circuits identity validation and returns `("agent", "author_test")`. Then `_dispatch_attested` emits `step_attested` (line 941-943) and runs inline checks.
     f. After each loop iteration, the events file has grown — next iteration's `peek_current_step` will see the new events.

5. **Cleanup**: Use `try/finally`:
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to original value (or `pop` if not originally set).
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` to original value.
   - If `ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH=1` is set, print the scratch path and skip deletion; otherwise `shutil.rmtree` the temp directory.

6. **Edge cases**:
   - If the plan has no steps, `peek_current_step` returns exhausted immediately (gate.py:475-476).
   - Code step subprocess failure: nonzero returncode is recorded in `step_completed` but does not halt the run. `peek_current_step` advances the cursor past it.
   - `cmd_start` might fail if `active_run.json` already exists for the slug. The scratch dir is fresh, so this won't happen. If the slug is reused across calls, `shutil.rmtree` before `cmd_start`.

## Phase 3: Wire `author test` CLI

### Step 4: Rewrite `_cmd_test` and update parser in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Medium, replace Phase 5 scaffold logic with dual-path (~120 lines changed/added)

1. **Parser change** (`_build_parser`, line 462-471):
   - Keep the existing `test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", ...)` for backward compat (scaffold tests pass `["test", "demo.app", "--fixture", "f1"]`).
   - Add optional flags:
     ```python
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None, help="pack name (e.g. 'builtin')")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None, help="orchestrator name (e.g. 'hype')")
     ```
     The `qualified_id` positional remains required in argparse (for backward compat), but in dispatch we resolve the effective qid from either form.
   - Keep `test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, ...)`.
   - Add `test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", ...)`.

2. **Dispatch** (in `main`, line 491-492):
   - If `args.pack` and `args.orchestrator` are both set: use `qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"` as the effective qualified id.
   - Otherwise: use `args.qualified_id` (the positional).
   -urne `_cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, args.regenerate, packs_root)`.

3. **New `_cmd_test` signature and body**:
   ```python
   def _cmd_test(qid: str, fixture_name: str, regenerate: bool, packs_root: Optional[Path]) -> int
   ```
   - Resolve `root = _packs_root_arg(packs_root)`, `pack, name = _qualified_split(qid)`, `pack_root = root / pack`.
   - Check for compiled plan at `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json`.
   - **Path A: Runtime replay** (compiled plan exists):
     a. Load `plan_json = json.loads(build_path.read_text())`.
     b. Load fixture dir at `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` (must exist, return 2 if missing).
     c. Call `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json, fixture_dir, pack, name, root)` → `(events_path, run_dir)`.
     d. Normalize captured events: `captured = normalize_events_jsonl(events_path, run_dir)`.
     e. Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     f. If `--regenerate`:
        - Write `captured` as normalized JSON lines to golden path (one JSON object per line).
        - Print: "regenerated golden at <golden_path> — commit this file if the change is intentional".
        - Return 0.
     g. If golden missing/empty AND not `--regenerate`:
        - Print: "no golden at <golden_path>; run with --regenerate to capture the first golden".
        - Return 2.
     h. Otherwise: load golden, normalize each line (using the same `run_dir` for path replacement), diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.
   - **Path B: Phase 5 scaffold fallback** (no compiled plan):
     - This path is reached when `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json` does not exist.
     - Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - Captured: `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/events.jsonl`.
     - Check golden exists and non-empty — if not, print Phase 9 message and return 2.
     - Check captured exists — if not, print message and return 2.
     - Diff using `normalize_line` (from normalize.py, not `_strip_volatile`). Return 0/1.

4. **Remove `_strip_volatile`**: Delete the function and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant (~lines 287-308). Import `normalize_line` from `normalize.py` instead. Update the Path B fallback code to use `normalize_line`.

## Phase 4: Golden Fixture for Smoke Test

### Step 5: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` — DSL smoke orchestrator
**Scope:** Small, new file (~25 lines)

1. **Implement** a minimal DSL orchestrator in the `builtin` pack:
   ```python
   """Smoke-test orchestrator for Phase 9 golden runs.

   Reads input from ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR (set by author test runner) and
   writes a produces file that passes an inline file_nonempty check.
   """
   import sys
   from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, code, file_nonempty

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code(
               "smoke_step",
               argv=[sys.executable, "-c",
                     "import json, os; "
                     "fixture_dir = os.environ.get('ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR', '.'); "
                     "json.dump({'ok': True}, open(os.path.join(fixture_dir, 'out.json'), 'w'))"],
               produces={"out": file_nonempty()},
           ),
       ]
   ```
   The `code()` DSL function accepts `argv` as a list — this becomes `step.command` as a shell-quoted string in the compiled plan. The subprocess writes an output file to `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` which the runner populated from the fixture directory. `file_nonempty()` is an inline check that verifies the file exists and is non-empty.

### Step 6: Create fixtures directory and compile/regenerate golden
**Scope:** Small, new files + shell commands

1. **`artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/`**: Create `.keep` marker file (minimal fixture — the smoke orchestrator just needs the directory to exist).

2. **`artagents/packs/builtin/golden/`**: Create directory if it doesn't exist.

3. **Compile** the orchestrator:
   ```
   artagents author compile builtin.hype
   ```
   This writes `artagents/packs/builtin/build/hype.json` (gitignored per .gitignore:60 — must be regenerated on fresh checkout).

4. **Regenerate golden**:
   ```
   artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate
   ```
   This executes the runtime replay, normalizes the events, and writes `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.

5. **Commit** the golden file. The `build/hype.json` is gitignored and NOT committed — the acceptance command must run `compile` first.

## Phase 5: Tests

### Step 7: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~60 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_pass`:
   - Create a temp pack directory under a tmp_path with a `.py` DSL orchestrator (one code step, no attested steps — simplest pass case).
   - Compile it via `compile_to_path`.
   - Create `fixtures/f1/` with a `.keep`.
   - Run the runner to produce a golden via `--regenerate`.
   - Run `author test --pack <pack> --orchestrator <name> --fixture f1`.
   - Assert exit code 0 and stdout contains "ok".

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~60 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_drift_reported`:
   - Same setup as the pass test but after generating the golden, corrupt one event's structural field (e.g., change `returncode` from 0 to 1).
   - Run `author test`. Assert exit 1 and stdout contains unified diff lines.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~50 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_regenerate_creates_golden`:
   - Set up as above but skip golden generation.
   - Run `author test --regenerate`.
   - Assert golden file now exists.
   - Run `author test` (without --regenerate). Assert exit 0.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~80 lines)

1. **Test 1**: `test_auto_approval_with_env_var_and_identity`:
   - Create a temp plan with one attested step (agent ack). Compile it.
   - Run the runtime replay (with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` set).
   - Load the resulting `events.jsonl`.
   - Assert it contains a `step_attested` event with `attestor_id: "author_test"` and `attestor_kind: "agent"`.

2. **Test 2**: `test_auto_approval_rejected_without_env_var`:
   - Same plan. Run with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` unset (or set to "0").
   - The `run_plan_in_scratch` loop will fail because `cmd_ack` cannot approve without identity validation (the test expects an exception or the plan loop to block).

3. **Test 3**: `test_env_leakage_without_identity_is_inert`:
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` but send an ack with `--agent real_agent` (not `author_test`).
   - Verify `validate_attested_identity` still runs normally (i.e., rejects if `real_agent` doesn't pass real identity validation).

### Step 11: Verify no regressions in existing tests
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/ -x -q` to ensure all existing tests pass.
2. If there are scaffold test files referencing `demo.app`, they use Path B (no compiled plan) and should pass unchanged because `_strip_volatile` is replaced by `normalize_line` which is a superset.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (normalize.py) — foundation, no side effects
2. Step 2 (auto-approval gate + env propagation) — tiny targeted changes
3. Step 3 (runner.py) — core replay engine, uses `gate_command` + `peek_current_step` + `cmd_ack` + `record_dispatch_complete`
4. Step 4 (CLI dual-path + retire `_strip_volatile`) — wires it all together
5. Step 5 (builtin/hype.py smoke orchestrator)
6. Step 6 (fixture dirs + compile + regenerate golden)
7. Steps 7-10 (new Phase 9 tests)
8. Step 11 (regression — full suite)

## Validation Order

1. `artagents author compile builtin.hype` — must succeed before the acceptance command (build/ is gitignored)
2. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` — bootstrap the golden
3. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — literal stop-condition acceptance command
4. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py tests/test_author_test_drift.py tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py -v` — new Phase 9 tests
5. `pytest tests/ -x -q` — full suite, no regressions

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 5)

## Overview

Phase 9 delivers `artagents author test`: a runtime replay path that starts a task in a scratch directory, auto-approves attested steps, executes code steps via subprocess, captures the event stream, normalizes volatile fields, and diffs against a golden file. The Phase 5 scaffold path (file-diff of pre-written captured files at `fixtures/<name>/events.jsonl`) is retained as a backward-compatible fallback when no compiled plan exists at `<pack>/build/<name>.json`.

**Source-verified decisions (unchanged from v4, confirmed against repo)**:
- `CodeStep.command` is a `str` (plan.py:60). The runner uses `shlex.split(step.command)` for subprocess argv.
- `_dispatch_code` (gate.py:790-860) emits `step_dispatched` (line 847), applies `ARTAGENTS_TASK_*` env vars (line 848), and handles reentry (lines 807-845). The runner MUST call `gate_command` before subprocess.
- `cmd_ack._ack_approve` (lifecycle_ack.py:190-228) constructs the incoming command as `step.command + " --agent author_test"`. `match_attested_command` (gate.py:1025-1061) strips identity tokens and compares the remainder to `step.command`. The flow is correct for programmatic invocation with `--agent author_test`.
- `artagents/packs/*/build/` is gitignored (.gitignore:60). The compiled `build/hype.json` must be regenerated on fresh checkout via `artagents author compile builtin.hype`.
- `child_subprocess_env` (env.py:68-81) must propagate `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST`. Added to key list.

**Resolved from critique v4 (issue_hints-1, FLAG-CR-016, FLAG-CR-017, correctness, scope, all_locations)**:
- **The `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` folder DOES exist** with `orchestrator.yaml`, `run.py`, `__init__.py`, `STAGE.md`. The plan's v4 assumption that it didn't exist was factually wrong. However, `resolve_orchestrator` (compile.py:73-114) loads `<name>.py` via `_load_module_isolated` using a unique module name — it never does a plain `import artagents.packs.builtin.hype`, so the Python package-shadow issue does NOT break compilation or runtime. `_cmd_new` at cli.py:257-263 WILL reject `author new builtin.hype` because of the folder-collision check; the DSL smoke orchestrator is created manually (not via `author new`). No other author verb does plain imports of `builtin.hype` — they all go through `resolve_orchestrator` → isolated loading, so they work correctly. The legacy yaml orchestrator is invoked via `artagents.packs.builtin.hype.run` (orchestrator.yaml:30), which is unaffected by the presence of a `.py` file.
- **The produces-dir bug (FLAG-CR-017)**: The smoke orchestrator was writing `out.json` to `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` (which maps to `run_dir/fixture_inputs/`), but `_run_inline_checks` (gate.py:1111-1170) checks `step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, plan_step_path, ...)` / `entry.path` — the step-specific directory at `<run_dir>/steps/<step_id>/`. The fix: the smoke orchestrator writes `out.json` to its CWD, and the runner sets `cwd` to the step directory (computed via `step_dir_for_path`) before running the subprocess. The produces path is `"out.json"` (relative), which resolves to `<step_dir>/out.json` — exactly where the check looks.
- **Smoke fixture**: `artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/` — a `.keep` marker file (the smoke orchestrator just needs the directory to exist; it reads no fixture inputs).
- **Golden file**: `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — committed, NOT gitignored. Bootstrap via `--regenerate`.
- **Phase 5 scaffold tests** (`tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`) pass unchanged because the dual-path strategy routes them through Path B (no compiled plan → Phase 5 file-diff fallback using `normalize_line` instead of `_strip_volatile`).

## Phase 1: Foundation — Normalization + Auto-Approval + env propagation

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/normalize.py` — event normalization
**Scope:** Small, new file (~130 lines)

1. **Implement** `NORMALIZE_STRIP_FIELDS = ("ts", "hash", "run_id", "pid")` — fields to drop entirely during normalization.

2. **Implement** `normalize_event(event: dict, run_dir: Path) -> dict`:
   - Drop keys in `NORMALIZE_STRIP_FIELDS`.
   - Replace absolute paths: for any string value whose `str` representation starts with `str(run_dir)`, replace the prefix with `<RUN_DIR>` and append the relative remainder. Also replace `str(run_dir.parent)` (project root) with `<PROJECT_DIR>`.
   - Keep all structural fields: `kind`, `plan_step_id`, `plan_step_path`, `command`, `returncode`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`, `evidence`, `check_id`, `produces_name`, `cas_sha256`, `iteration`, `item_id`, `reason`, `on_exhaust`, `max_iterations`, `child_plan_hash`, `actor`, `plan_hash`, `item_ids`, `source`, `path_tuple`.

3. **Implement** `normalize_events_jsonl(path: Path, run_dir: Path) -> list[dict]`:
   - Read lines, JSON-decode each, pass through `normalize_event`, return list.

4. **Implement** `normalize_line(line: str, run_dir: Path) -> str`:
   - Thin wrapper: JSON-decodes, normalizes, re-encodes with `sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False`. Returns raw line on decode failure (so malformed lines surface in diff).

5. **Document**: Hash-chain hashes (`hash` field) are stripped (not recomputed) because they depend on `ts` which is volatile. Structural fields alone are sufficient for diffing event drift. `ts` is stripped because it's a UTC timestamp that varies per run. `run_id` is stripped because the runner generates a fresh one each time. `pid` is stripped because OS PIDs vary per process.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval bypass to `validate_attested_identity` in `artagents/core/task/gate.py`
**Scope:** Tiny, modify existing function (~7 lines)

1. **Add early return** right after the mutually-exclusive check at line 1074 (before the `step.ack.kind` branch at line 1075):
   ```python
   # Auto-approval for author test: requires BOTH env var AND synthetic identity.
   if os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST") == "1":
       if args.agent == "author_test" or args.actor == "author_test":
           kind = "agent" if args.agent == "author_test" else "actor"
           return kind, "author_test"
   ```
   This double-gates auto-approval on both the env var AND the synthetic identity `author_test`. An env leak alone cannot bypass identity validation. The `task_actor_env() != args.actor` check at line 1082 is never reached for actor-ack steps under this bypass, so `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` does not need to be set.

2. **Add `import os`** to gate.py imports if not already present (it is at the top of the file for path operations but verify it's present for `os.environ`).

### Step 3: Propagate `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` through `child_subprocess_env`
**Scope:** Tiny, modify `artagents/core/task/env.py` (~1 line change)

1. **In `child_subprocess_env`** (env.py:68-81), add `"ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` to the tuple of keys copied from parent environ at line 71-77, alongside `TASK_RUN_ID_ENV`, `TASK_PROJECT_ENV`, etc.

## Phase 2: Runtime Replay Engine

### Step 4: Create `artagents/orchestrate/author_test_runner.py` — runtime replay
**Scope:** Medium, new file (~220 lines)

1. **Implement** `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json: dict, fixture_dir: Path, pack: str, name: str, packs_root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]`:
   - Returns `(events_jsonl_path, run_dir)`.
   - Create temp directory via `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="author_test_")` as scratch root.
   - Inside scratch, create `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/build/` and write `plan_json` to `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/build/<name>.json`. Also create `<scratch>/packs/<pack>/` directory (needed for `_resolve_packs_root`).
   - Use project slug `author_test` (validated per `validate_project_slug`). Ensure no stale state: `shutil.rmtree` the project dir if it exists.
   - Set `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"] = "1"`. Save the original value (or absence) and restore in `finally`.
   - Call `cmd_start` (imported from `artagents.core.task.lifecycle`):
     ```python
     rc = cmd_start([f"{pack}.{name}", "--project", slug], packs_root=scratch_packs, projects_root=scratch_projects)
     ```
     `cmd_start` (lifecycle.py:130-220) reads the compiled plan from `<packs_root>/<pack>/build/<name>.json` (line 162), writes `plan.json` (line 179), creates run dir (line 198-199), writes `active_run.json` (line 201), and appends `run_started` event (line 205).
   - After `cmd_start`, read `active_run.json` to get `run_id`, compute `run_dir = project_dir(slug, root=scratch_projects) / "runs" / run_id`.
   - Copy fixture files: `fixture_dir` contents → `run_dir / "fixture_inputs" /`. Set `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR"] = str(run_dir / "fixture_inputs")` and restore on cleanup.

2. **Drive the plan loop** using `peek_current_step` + `gate_command` + `cmd_ack`:
   - Loop variables: `proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=scratch_projects)`, `plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"`, `events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"`.
   - Loop:
     a. Reload `plan = load_plan(plan_path)` and `events = read_events(events_path)` from disk.
     b. Call `peek = peek_current_step(plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id)` (gate.py:454-507).
     c. If `peek.exhausted` or `peek.step is None` → break.
     d. **If `isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep)`**:
        - Call `gate_command` to emit `step_dispatched` and apply `ARTAGENTS_TASK_*` env vars:
          ```python
          decision = gate_command(slug, peek.step.command, [], root=scratch_projects)
          ```
        - Compute step directory:
          ```python
          step_dir = step_dir_for_path(slug, run_id, peek.path_tuple, iteration=peek.iteration, item_id=peek.item_id, root=scratch_projects)
          step_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          ```
        - Run the subprocess in the step directory:
          ```python
          cmd_tokens = shlex.split(peek.step.command)
          proc = subprocess.run(cmd_tokens, cwd=step_dir, env=child_subprocess_env(), capture_output=True)
          ```
          The CWD is `step_dir` so produces files written to CWD are found by the inline check.
        - Call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, proc.returncode)` (gate.py:1096-1107) which emits `step_completed` (line 1104) and runs inline produces checks (line 1106-1107).
     e. **If `isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep)`**:
        - Build ack argv based on `peek.step.ack.kind`:
          - If `"agent"`: `ack_argv = [STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple), "--project", slug, "--decision", "approve", "--agent", "author_test"]`
          - If `"actor"`: `ack_argv = [..., "--actor", "author_test"]`
        - Include `["--item", peek.item_id]` if `peek.item_id` is set.
        - Call `cmd_ack(ack_argv, projects_root=scratch_projects)` (lifecycle_ack.py:102-187).
     f. After each iteration, events file grows — next `peek_current_step` sees the new events.

3. **Cleanup** (in `finally` block):
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` to original value (or `pop` if not originally set).
   - Restore `ARTAGENTS_FIXTURE_DIR` to original value.
   - If `os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_KEEP_SCRATCH") == "1"`: print scratch path and skip deletion. Otherwise: `shutil.rmtree(scratch_dir)`.

4. **Edge cases**:
   - Empty plan: `peek_current_step` returns exhausted immediately (gate.py:475-476).
   - Code step subprocess failure: nonzero returncode recorded in `step_completed`, cursor advances.
   - `cmd_start` failure if slug reused: `shutil.rmtree` before calling.
   - `gate_command` may raise `TaskRunGateError` — let it propagate to caller.

## Phase 3: Wire `author test` CLI

### Step 5: Rewrite `_cmd_test` and update parser in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`
**Scope:** Medium, replace Phase 5 scaffold logic with dual-path (~120 lines changed/added)

1. **Parser changes** (`_build_parser`, starting at line 462):
   - Keep the existing `test_p.add_argument("qualified_id", ...)` for backward compat (scaffold tests pass `["test", "demo.app", "--fixture", "f1"]`).
   - Add optional flags:
     ```python
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", default=None, help="pack name (e.g. 'builtin')")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", default=None, help="orchestrator name (e.g. 'hype')")
     ```
   - Keep `test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, ...)`.
   - Add `test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", help="rewrite the golden file from the current run")`.

2. **Dispatch** (in `main`, lines 491-492):
   - If `args.pack` and `args.orchestrator` are both set: `qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"`.
   - Otherwise: `qid = args.qualified_id`.
   - Call `_cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, args.regenerate, packs_root)`.

3. **New `_cmd_test` signature and body**:
   ```python
   def _cmd_test(qid: str, fixture_name: str, regenerate: bool, packs_root: Optional[Path]) -> int
   ```
   - Resolve `root = _packs_root_arg(packs_root)`, `pack, name = _qualified_split(qid)`, `pack_root = root / pack`.
   - Check for compiled plan at `<pack_root>/build/<name>.json`.
   - **Path A: Runtime replay** (compiled plan exists):
     a. Load `plan_json = json.loads(build_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))`.
     b. Fixture dir at `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/` must exist and be a directory (return 2 if missing).
     c. Call `run_plan_in_scratch(plan_json, fixture_dir, pack, name, root)` → `(events_path, run_dir)`.
     d. Normalize captured events: `captured = normalize_events_jsonl(events_path, run_dir)`.
     e. Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     f. If `--regenerate`:
        - Write `captured` as normalized JSON lines to golden path (one JSON object per line, `sort_keys=True`).
        - Print: `"regenerated golden at <golden_path> — commit this file if the change is intentional"`.
        - Return 0.
     g. If golden missing/empty AND not `--regenerate`:
        - Print: `"no golden at <golden_path>; run with --regenerate to capture the first golden"`.
        - Return 2.
     h. Otherwise: load golden, normalize each line (using the same `run_dir` for path replacement), diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Return 0 on match, 1 on drift.
   - **Path B: Phase 5 scaffold fallback** (no compiled plan):
     - Golden path: `<pack_root>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - Captured: `<pack_root>/fixtures/<fixture_name>/events.jsonl`.
     - Check golden exists and non-empty — if not, print original Phase 9 message ("implement Phase 9 to capture golden runs") and return 2.
     - Check captured exists — if not, print message and return 2.
     - Diff using `normalize_line` from `normalize.py` (passing `run_dir=fixture_dir` for path replacement). Return 0/1.

4. **Remove `_strip_volatile`**: Delete the function and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant (~lines 287-308). Import `normalize_line` from `normalize.py`. Update the Path B fallback code and all `_strip_volatile` callsites to use `normalize_line`.

## Phase 4: Golden Fixture for Smoke Test

### Step 6: Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` — DSL smoke orchestrator
**Scope:** Small, new file (~30 lines)

1. **Implement** a minimal DSL orchestrator in the `builtin` pack (alongside the existing `builtin/hype/` folder):
   ```python
   """Smoke-test orchestrator for Phase 9 golden runs.

   Writes a produces file that passes an inline file_nonempty check.
   The author-test runner sets CWD to the step directory, so writing
   out.json there makes the check find it.
   """
   import json
   import sys
   from artagents.orchestrate import orchestrator, code, file_nonempty

   @orchestrator("builtin.hype")
   def hype():
       return [
           code(
               "smoke_step",
               argv=[sys.executable, "-c",
                     "import json; json.dump({'ok': True}, open('out.json', 'w'))"],
               produces={"out": file_nonempty()},
           ),
       ]
   ```
   Key points:
   - The `code()` argv uses `sys.executable` for portability.
   - The inline Python writes `out.json` to CWD (the step directory set by the runner).
   - `file_nonempty()` with default path (derived from produces name `"out"` → `"out"`) looks for `out.json` in the step directory — exactly where the subprocess wrote it.
   - This file coexists with the `builtin/hype/` folder. `resolve_orchestrator` loads it via `_load_module_isolated` which explicitly opens `<pack_root>/<name>.py` (compile.py:86) — the folder does NOT shadow isolated loading.

### Step 7: Create fixture directories and bootstrap golden
**Scope:** Small, new files + shell commands

1. **`artagents/packs/builtin/fixtures/smoke/`**: Create directory with a `.keep` marker file. The smoke orchestrator does not read fixture inputs; the directory just needs to exist.

2. **`artagents/packs/builtin/golden/`**: Create directory if it doesn't already exist.

3. **Compile** the DSL orchestrator:
   ```
   artagents author compile builtin.hype
   ```
   This writes `artagents/packs/builtin/build/hype.json` (gitignored — regenerated on fresh checkout).

4. **Bootstrap the golden** via regenerate:
   ```
   artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate
   ```
   This executes runtime replay, normalizes events, and writes `artagents/packs/builtin/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`.

5. **Commit** the golden file and fixture directory. The `build/hype.json` is gitignored and NOT committed.

## Phase 5: Tests

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~65 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_pass_against_committed_golden`:
   - Create a temp pack under `tmp_path` with a `.py` DSL orchestrator (one code step using `sys.executable -c "..."`).
   - Compile it via `compile_to_path`.
   - Create `fixtures/f1/` with `.keep`.
   - Bootstrap golden via `_cmd_test("<pack>.<name>", "f1", regenerate=True, packs_root)`.
   - Run `_cmd_test("<pack>.<name>", "f1", regenerate=False, packs_root)`.
   - Assert exit code 0.

2. **Test**: `test_author_test_pass_with_attested_step`:
   - Same setup but with one attested step (agent ack) instead of code step.
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` before running.
   - Assert the run completes (auto-approval fires) and golden matches.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~60 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_drift_reports_diff`:
   - Same setup as pass test. Bootstrap golden.
   - Corrupt one event's structural field (e.g., change `returncode` from 0 to 1 in the golden).
   - Run `author test`. Assert exit 1 and stdout contains unified diff lines ("---", "+++", the changed field).

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~55 lines)

1. **Test**: `test_author_test_regenerate_creates_or_overwrites_golden`:
   - Set up pack, compile, create fixture dir, but skip golden creation.
   - Run `author test --regenerate`. Assert golden file exists and is non-empty.
   - Run `author test` (no --regenerate). Assert exit 0.
   - Corrupt golden, run `--regenerate` again, assert golden is rewritten and test passes.

### Step 11: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Small, new file (~90 lines)

1. **Test 1**: `test_auto_approval_with_env_and_identity`:
   - Create temp plan with one attested step (agent ack). Compile it.
   - Run `run_plan_in_scratch` with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`.
   - Load resulting `events.jsonl`.
   - Assert it contains a `step_attested` event with `attestor_id: "author_test"` and `attestor_kind: "agent"`.

2. **Test 2**: `test_auto_approval_rejected_without_env`:
   - Same plan. Run with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST` unset or "0".
   - `cmd_ack` will fail because `validate_attested_identity` rejects the fake `author_test` identity without the env var. Assert an exception is raised or the return code is non-zero.

3. **Test 3**: `test_env_leakage_without_identity_is_inert`:
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` but send an ack with `--agent real_agent` (not `author_test`).
   - The auto-approval bypass only fires when the identity IS `author_test`. Assert `validate_attested_identity` runs normally (rejects if `real_agent` doesn't pass real identity validation).

### Step 12: Verify no regressions in existing tests
**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `pytest tests/ -x -q` to ensure all existing tests pass.
2. **Phase 5 scaffold tests** (`tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`) use Path B (no compiled plan) and pass unchanged because `normalize_line` from `normalize.py` is a superset of the old `_strip_volatile` (strips `ts`, `hash` AND also `run_id`, `pid`, abs paths — which the synthetic scaffold data doesn't have).
3. **Author CLI tests** (`tests/test_author_cli.py`) are unaffected — they exercise `compile`, `check`, `describe`, `new`, not `test`.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (normalize.py) — foundation, no side effects
2. Step 2 (auto-approval gate bypass) — tiny targeted change in gate.py
3. Step 3 (env propagation) — 1-line change in env.py
4. Step 4 (runner.py) — core replay engine, depends on Steps 1-3
5. Step 5 (CLI dual-path + retire `_strip_volatile`) — depends on Steps 1, 4
6. Step 6 (builtin/hype.py) — DSL smoke orchestrator
7. Step 7 (fixture dirs + compile + bootstrap golden) — depends on Steps 5, 6
8. Steps 8-11 (new Phase 9 tests) — depend on Steps 5-7
9. Step 12 (regression) — full suite after all changes

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_author_cli.py tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py -v` — existing tests still pass after `_strip_volatile` removed
2. `artagents author compile builtin.hype` — must succeed (build/ is gitignored)
3. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate` — bootstrap the golden
4. `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — literal stop-condition acceptance command
5. `pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py tests/test_author_test_drift.py tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py -v` — new Phase 9 tests
6. `pytest tests/ -x -q` — full suite, no regressions

    "idea": "Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).\n\nLAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.\n\nDRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.\n\n\nPHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):\n\n- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.\n- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).\n- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.\n\nEXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):\n- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.\n\nWHAT TO IMPLEMENT:\n\n1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):\n   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`\n   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.\n   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` \u2014 fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.\n   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).\n   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths \u2014 keep only structural fields).\n   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.\n   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.\n\n2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):\n   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.\n   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow \u2014 only `author test` sets the env var.\n\n3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):\n   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).\n   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.\n   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) \u2014 recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.\n\n4. Regenerate workflow:\n   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.\n   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.\n\n5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` \u2014 minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` \u2014 the expected event sequence.\n\n6. Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.\n- No new dependencies (stdlib only).\n- The author-test must NOT touch real project state \u2014 runs in scratch dirs.\n- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.\n\nSTOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.",

        "id": "FLAG-CR-001",
        "concern": "CLI signature mismatch: stop condition requires --pack/--orchestrator/--fixture flags, plan retains positional <pack>.<name>. Acceptance command will not parse.",
        "resolution": "Revised plan to address all 15 significant critique flags: (1) CLI parser changed from positional qualified_id to --pack/--orchestrator/--fixture flags matching the acceptance command. (2) Phase 5 scaffold tests preserved via a dual-path strategy \u2014 runtime replay when compiled plan exists, Phase 5 file-diff fallback when it doesn't, so pre-written captured files still work. (3) Orchestrator placed in new pack 'builtin_golden' to avoid collision with existing yaml-defined builtin/hype/ folder. (4) Fixture files placed in run_dir/fixture_inputs/ accessible via env var, not pre-populated into produces dirs. (5) Auto-approval narrowed to validate_attested_identity with double-gate (env var + synthetic author_test identity). (6) Hardcoded python3 replaced with sys.executable. (7) Replay engine reuses cmd_start/cmd_next/cmd_ack production entry points instead of reimplementing the dispatch loop. (8) _strip_volatile retired in favor of normalize.py. (9) ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST propagated via child_subprocess_env. (10) Synthetic command uses --agent or --actor based on ack.kind. (11) Success criteria updated with proper requires fields."
      },

        "id": "FLAG-CR-002",

        Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```
